03:11:56 --> 03:12:01
from napoleon hill

03:11:58 --> 03:12:03
if you cannot do great things

03:12:00 --> 03:12:05
do small things

03:12:02 --> 03:12:07
in a great way

03:12:04 --> 03:12:11
thank you for listening and hope to see

03:12:06 --> 03:12:11
you next time

<!-- YOUTUBE_TRANSCRIPT_END -->
